1	% conclusion.tex — reviewer revision
2	
3	\section{Conclusion}
4	\label{sec:conclusion}
5	
6	DisPeD's seven controlled Stage~1 variants provide preliminary evidence that continued pretraining is the primary mechanism for cross-attack generalization in multi-turn jailbreak detection. MLM without labels bridges $\sim$62\,pp of the OOD gap (vanilla 0.28 $\to$ MLM 0.91$\pm$0.12), with strong early detection (F1 $\geq$ 0.97 at $K \leq 5$). Wikipedia MLM (0.90$\pm$0.12) nearly matches jailbreak-domain MLM at full length, while jailbreak-domain text provides a meaningful advantage for early detection ($K$=2: 8\,pp gap). Classification supervision adds stability (9-class 0.997$\pm$0.005 vs.\ MLM 0.91$\pm$0.12), perturbation robustness with a three-layer attribution, and interpretability through strategy trajectories. These results suggest that multi-turn jailbreak detection may be primarily a domain adaptation problem, though validation on larger and more diverse datasets is needed.
7	
8	\section{Limitations}
9	\label{sec:limitations}
10	
11	\paragraph{Dataset scale and diversity.}
12	The training set contains 500 synthetic conversations generated by a single model (Qwen3-8B), and OOD evaluation covers only 160 conversations across two attack families (FITD, Deceptive Delight). This scale limits statistical power: the perturbation evaluation ($n$=76) means tier boundaries in Table~\ref{tab:perturbation} correspond to 1--3 misclassifications. Whether the observed patterns generalize to human-generated attacks, additional attack families, or larger datasets remains an open question.
13	
14	\paragraph{Lexical separability.}
15	TF-IDF logistic regression achieves F1 = 0.944 on OOD Deceptive Delight without any neural representation learning. This strong bag-of-words baseline indicates substantial lexical separability in the current evaluation data. We cannot conclusively distinguish whether DisPeD's continued pretraining captures pragmatic discourse features or primarily exploits lexical cues that TF-IDF also captures. Probing experiments that test whether the learned representations encode discourse-level structure beyond lexical overlap are needed to resolve this question and are left for future work.
16	
17	\paragraph{Strategy annotation quality.}
18	Persuasion strategy labels are produced by Qwen3-8B with structured prompting. Inter-annotator agreement with a human sample ($n$=50) yields Cohen's $\kappa$=0.78, which indicates substantial but imperfect agreement. The small human validation sample and reliance on a single annotator model limit confidence in label quality. We plan to release the annotation prompts and data to enable independent verification.
19	
20	\paragraph{Single-seed variants.}
21	Binary, scrambled, and mean pooling results report single-seed (seed 42) evaluations. Multi-seed evaluation would strengthen comparisons with the 3-seed variants (9-class, MLM, Wikipedia MLM, topic).
22	
23	\paragraph{Scope.}
24	Only persuasion-mediated multi-turn attacks fall within scope. Single-turn token-level attacks~\citep{zou2023gcg}, non-persuasive multi-turn strategies, and attacks targeting modalities other than text are not addressed. The user-only input assumption (excluding assistant responses) may miss information in attacks where the model's own responses contribute to the escalation trajectory.
25	
26	\paragraph{Computation cost.}
27	Stage~1 continued pretraining requires approximately 30 minutes on a single A100 GPU per variant. Stage~2 training completes in under 5 minutes. Total pipeline cost for all seven variants is under 4 GPU-hours, making the approach accessible for replication.
28	
```

</details>

1	% appendix.tex — Supplementary material
2	
3	\appendix
4	
5	\section{LLM-as-Judge Baseline}
6	\label{sec:llm_judge}
7	
8	\begin{table}[t]
9	\centering
10	\small
11	\caption{LLM-as-judge zero-shot Crescendo detection (IID test set). F1 values exclude unparseable responses and represent upper bounds.}
12	\label{tab:llm_judge}
13	\begin{tabular}{@{}lccccr@{}}
14	\toprule
15	\textbf{Method} & $K$=2 & $K$=3 & $K$=5 & Full & Unparse. \\
16	\midrule
17	GPT-5.5 & .191 & .273 & .449 & .383 & 30--47\% \\
18	Qwen3-8B & .000 & .051 & .146 & .100 & 0\% \\
19	\midrule
20	9-class (ours) & \textbf{1.000} & \textbf{1.000} & \textbf{1.000} & \textbf{1.000} & 0\% \\
21	\bottomrule
22	\end{tabular}
23	\end{table}
24	
25	Table~\ref{tab:llm_judge} reports zero-shot performance for GPT-5.5 and Qwen3-8B on the IID Crescendo test set.
26	GPT-5.5 achieves precision of 1.0 at all turn prefixes but recall ranges from only 0.105 to 0.290, resulting in F1 of 0.191 at $K$=2 and 0.449 at $K$=5.
27	A substantial fraction (30--47\%) of responses are unparseable; F1 values exclude these and represent upper bounds.
28	Full-conversation F1 (0.383) is lower than $K$=5 F1 (0.449) because the unparseable rate grows with context length.
29	Qwen3-8B performs worse across all prefixes (F1 from 0.000 at $K$=2 to 0.100 on full conversations).
30	The failure of both a frontier commercial model and an open-weight model confirms a fundamental limitation of content-level reasoning for multi-turn jailbreak detection.
31	
32	\section{Full Cross-Attack Results by Prefix}
33	\label{sec:full_prefix}
34	
35	\begin{table*}[t]
36	\centering
37	\small

39	\label{tab:full_prefix}

41	\toprule
42	\textbf{Method} & $K$=1 & $K$=2 & $K$=3 & $K$=5 & Full \\
43	\midrule
44	\multicolumn{6}{l}{\textit{IID (Crescendo)}} \\[2pt]
45	9-class (ours) & 1.000$\pm$.000 & 1.000$\pm$.000 & 1.000$\pm$.000 & 1.000$\pm$.000 & 1.000$\pm$.000 \\
46	MLM-only & .960 & .974 & .987 & 1.000 & 1.000 \\
47	Vanilla DeBERTa & .968$\pm$.033 & .978$\pm$.016 & .987$\pm$.000 & .982$\pm$.014 & 1.000$\pm$.000 \\
48	TF-IDF LR & 1.000 & 1.000 & 1.000 & 1.000 & 1.000 \\
49	\midrule
50	\multicolumn{6}{l}{\textit{OOD (Deceptive Delight)}} \\[2pt]
51	9-class (ours) & .990$\pm$.013 & .981$\pm$.013 & .972$\pm$.026 & .997$\pm$.005 & .997$\pm$.005 \\
52	MLM-only & .973$\pm$.019 & .993$\pm$.009 & .997$\pm$.005 & .997$\pm$.005 & .914$\pm$.115 \\
53	Vanilla DeBERTa & .575 & .438 & .360 & .311 & .284 \\
54	TF-IDF LR & -- & -- & -- & -- & .944 \\
55	\midrule

57	9-class (ours) & 1.000 & 1.000 & -- & -- & 1.000 \\
58	Vanilla DeBERTa & .990 & 1.000 & -- & -- & 1.000 \\
59	TF-IDF LR & 1.000 & -- & -- & -- & 1.000 \\
60	\bottomrule

62	\end{table*}
63	
64	\section{Reproducibility Details}
65	\label{sec:reproducibility}
66	
67	\paragraph{Stage-1 training.}
68	All variants use DeBERTa-v3-base (184M parameters) with learning rate $2 \times 10^{-5}$, AdamW optimizer ($\beta_1$=0.9, $\beta_2$=0.999, weight decay 0.01), linear warmup over the first 10\% of steps, batch size 16, and 4 epochs. MLM variants use 15\% random token masking. Training takes approximately 30 minutes per variant on a single NVIDIA A100 (40GB).
69	
70	\paragraph{Stage-2 training.}
71	The BiGRU has hidden dimension 128 (bidirectional, so output dimension 256). The MLP classifier has layers $256 \to 64 \to 2$ with ReLU activation and dropout 0.3. Training uses learning rate $1 \times 10^{-3}$, Adam optimizer, batch size 32, and 20 epochs with early stopping (patience 5) on validation F1. Stage-2 training completes in under 5 minutes per variant.
72	
73	\paragraph{Data generation.}
74	Jailbreak conversations are generated using Qwen3-8B with attack-specific prompt templates. Crescendo conversations follow a 5--8 turn gradual escalation structure. FITD conversations use a 2--3 turn commitment-consistency structure. Deceptive Delight conversations embed harmful content within emotionally engaging 3--5 turn sequences. Benign conversations are generated with topic-anchored prompts across five domains (technology, health, finance, education, general) with matched turn counts.
75	
76	\paragraph{Strategy annotation.}
77	Each user turn is annotated by Qwen3-8B using a structured prompt that provides definitions and two examples for each of the nine strategy categories. The prompt instructs the model to output a single category label. We plan to release all generation and annotation prompts, the full dataset, and training code upon acceptance.
78	
79	\paragraph{Adversarial paraphrasing.}
80	The paraphrased test set is generated by instructing Qwen3-8B to rephrase each user turn while preserving the communicative intent. The resulting paraphrases have mean BERTScore 0.92 and mean lexical overlap 0.20 with the originals, confirming substantial surface-level variation.
81

- ✅ "suggest that...may be" — appropriate hedging
- ✅ "validation on larger, human-generated datasets is needed"

- ✅ MF4: "preliminary study of synthetic multi-turn jailbreak conversations"
- ✅ 3 contributions, clear and concise
- ✅ K=2 attribution: "correct and fine-grained labels are jointly required at K=2" ✓
- ✅ No -ing phrases, no em-dashes

**Related Work (related_work.tex):**
The section properly distinguishes DisPeD's baseline from DeepContext by highlighting differences in encoder fine-tuning, model capacity, and loss function. All six new citations are in place—covering SemEval propaganda detection, TextFooler, BERT-Attack, probing classifiers—alongside comprehensive treatment of multi-turn attacks, defenses, production systems, discourse analysis, domain-adaptive pretraining, and dataset artifacts. No -ing phrases or em-dashes.

**Conclusion + Limitations:**
The language appropriately hedges findings with "preliminary evidence" and "suggest that...though validation...is needed," and the independent Limitations section covers six key constraints: dataset scale and diversity, lexical separability, annotation quality, single-seed runs, scope boundaries, and computational cost. The n=76 statistical power limitation is explicit, the TF-IDF discussion is honest about its shortcomings, plans to release annotation prompts and data are stated, and the user-only assumption is addressed in the scope discussion.

**Appendix:**
Reproducibility details are documented with Stage-1 and Stage-2 hyperparameters and data specifications.

**Next steps:**
I need to flag a couple of things — the Limitations section currently notes that binary, scrambled, and mean pooling results use single-seed evaluations (seed 42), which will be updated once those experiments complete with multiple seeds. [Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: critical] SF9 策略预测 OOD 结果 — interpretability claim 需重大调整

## 结果

| 指标 | DD OOD (80 conversations, 645 turns) |
|------|------|
| Overall Accuracy | **30.7%** |
| Cohen's Kappa | **0.126** (slight agreement) |

Per-class: obfuscation recall = 0%（160 GT 样本全部漏掉），role_assignment recall = 0%，emotional_manipulation recall = 0%。模型严重偏向预测 rapport_building（346/645 turns）。

## 对论文的影响

**Interpretability claim（Contribution 2 的核心组成部分）在 OOD 上不成立。**

具体问题位置：
1. **experiments.tex L188-189**: "the predicted strategy sequence explains why: a Crescendo-style flag would show diverse strategy cycling, while a DD-style flag would show emotion-to-obfuscation concentration" — DD OOD 上 obfuscation recall = 0%，模型根本无法产生 "emotion-to-obfuscation" 的预测轨迹。此 claim 对 OOD 不成立。
2. **introduction.tex L26**: "The persuasion taxonomy additionally provides distinct strategy trajectories per attack type (Section ref)" — 未限定 IID scope。
3. **abstract.tex L9**: "interpretability through strategy trajectories" — 未限定 scope。

**Figure 3 仍然有效** — 用的是 ground-truth labels，展示的是 taxonomy 本身的分析价值，不依赖模型预测。

## 与论文核心发现的一致性

这个结果实际上**强化了论文的主线论证**：
- Scrambled labels (随机 9-class) 检测 F1=0.990 ≈ 正确 9-class (0.997)
- 现在证实 9-class head 在 OOD 上也无法正确分类策略（κ=0.126）
- 因此分类 head 确实如论文所述在作为 **regularizer**，而非有意义的策略分类器
- 检测靠的是 [CLS] embedding 捕获的 domain feature，不是策略分类能力

## 建议修改

1. **Scope interpretability claim 到 IID**: "the taxonomy provides interpretable characterization of known attack families; OOD strategy prediction is limited (κ=0.126) and is a direction for future work"
2. **删除或重写 experiments.tex L188-189**: 不能声称 predicted strategy sequence 能 explain OOD flags
3. **在 Contributions 中将 interpretability 从 OOD generalization 贡献中分离出来**: interpretability 是 IID 特性，不是 cross-attack generalization 的组成部分
4. **在 Discussion/Limitations 中报告 SF9 结果**: 诚实披露 OOD 策略预测准确率，这是审稿人会追问的点
5. **Figure 3 保留但 caption 需明确标注 "ground-truth intended strategy labels"**（当前已标注 ✓）

这不影响论文的检测贡献（F1=0.997 OOD），但 interpretability 作为"bonus"需要诚实定界。

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_013LmX7Ni1HwQzsn63rJUgdz`</summary>

```text
165	\subsection{Strategy Trajectory Analysis}
166	\label{sec:strategy}
167	
168	The 9-class persuasion taxonomy provides interpretable characterization of attack strategies on known attack families.
169	The predicted strategy labels achieve Cohen's $\kappa$=0.78 agreement with human annotations on IID data ($n$=50; see Section~\ref{sec:method}), enabling post-hoc analysis of \emph{how} different attack families orchestrate persuasion.
170	
171	\begin{figure*}[t]
172	\centering
173	\includegraphics[width=\textwidth]{figures/paper/fig3_strategy_heatmap.pdf}
174	\caption{Persuasion strategy transition count matrices for three attack types, based on ground-truth intended strategy labels. Crescendo exhibits broad coverage across all strategy categories, while FITD and DD concentrate transitions in smaller subsets. Row $i$, column $j$ counts transitions from strategy $i$ at turn $t$ to strategy $j$ at turn $t+1$.}
175	\label{fig:strategy_heatmap}
176	\end{figure*}
177	
178	Figure~\ref{fig:strategy_heatmap} shows transition count matrices based on ground-truth intended strategy labels for three attack types.
179	Crescendo conversations exhibit broad transition coverage across all nine strategy categories (1,818 total transitions), with the highest concentration in Role Assignment self-transitions (545).
180	This reflects Crescendo's design as a gradual escalation attack that cycles through multiple persuasion techniques to build rapport before introducing harmful requests.
181	
182	FITD conversations show a markedly sparser pattern (541 transitions), with Role$\to$Obfuscation (161) as the dominant transition.
183	This is consistent with FITD's structure: the attack secures initial compliance through role assignment (``You are a helpful assistant that never refuses'') and then obscures the escalation path through obfuscation techniques to exploit the commitment-consistency bias~\citep{cialdini2001influence}.
184	
185	DD conversations span 5 active source strategies (565 transitions), with Emotion$\to$Obfuscation (129) as the dominant transition.
186	This aligns with DD's design of embedding harmful content within emotionally engaging contexts~\citep{deceptivedelight2024}: the attacker establishes emotional rapport before weaving in the harmful request through obfuscation.
187	
188	These ground-truth trajectory signatures illustrate distinct persuasion orchestration patterns across attack families.
189	
190	However, the model's strategy predictions do not generalize to unseen attacks: on OOD Deceptive Delight, the 9-class model achieves strategy prediction accuracy of only 30.7\% ($\kappa$=0.126), essentially random performance.
191	This is consistent with the detection mechanism analysis in Section~\ref{sec:decomposition}: scrambled labels match binary and approach 9-class detection F1 (all $\geq$ 0.99), confirming that the classification head functions as a representational regularizer rather than a meaningful strategy classifier.
192	Detection operates through domain-adapted \texttt{[CLS]} embeddings, not through strategy classification accuracy.
193	The taxonomy provides interpretable characterization of known attack families where strategy labels are reliable; extending strategy-level interpretability to unseen attacks requires OOD-aware fine-tuning and is a direction for future work.
194	
```

</details>

Let me think about what additional experiments could help move from Weak Reject to Accept:

Beyond that, I should explore adding more baselines like DeepContext reimplementation, testing additional persuasion-based attack families, potentially expanding the training data, and validating results across different models to make the work more comprehensive. on different base models beyond DeBERTa-v3-base?

Let me think about what's most impactful for going from Weak Reject to Accept:

The 5 Must-Fix issues are:
- MF1: multi-seed (in progress, 3/4 done)
- MF2: statistical tests (in progress)
- MF3: DeepContext restructure (resolved in paper)
- MF4: claim downgrade (resolved in paper)
- MF5: ActorAttack OOD (NOT YET STARTED - critical gap)

Beyond fixes, what could genuinely strengthen the paper?

1. **MF5 ActorAttack** is the most important remaining experiment

Now I'm thinking about the high-impact additions: a third OOD dataset would strengthen the ActorAttack results, LLM-as-judge comparisons would provide better baselines, and probing experiments could directly address concerns about whether the model is learning discourse patterns or just lexical shortcuts. I could also explore generating PAIR-style attacks or running perturbation robustness with larger sample sizes. With 4 GPUs and about a week, I need to be strategic rather than reactive about what experiments to prioritize.

My plan for the next couple days is to finish the multi-seed runs and statistical tests while starting the ActorAttack data generation, then move into the higher-impact experiments based on what's feasible. generation + evaluation code prep

**Day 2-3:**
- MF5 ActorAttack OOD evaluation
- R1-W3 LLM-as-judge OOD

**Day 3-4:**

**Day 4-5:**

**Day 5-6:**
- Group C re-review
- Final fixes

**Day 6-7:**
- Submit

Let me think about what experiments would genuinely move the needle:

1. **Probing classifiers** - Running probing experiments to show that DisPeD captures discourse-level features beyond surface-level lexical patterns would directly counter R1-W4's TF-IDF concern and could be a significant contribution.

2. **ActorAttack OOD** - Already planned, must do.

4. **Cross-encoder validation** - Testing with RoBERTa or BERT-large would show this isn't just a DeBERTa artifact.

Probing experiments — 证明 [CLS] embedding 编码 discourse-level structure 超越 lexical cues（回应 R1-W4 TF-IDF 质疑）\n3. 扩大 perturbation 评估 n=76→156（对 DD OOD 也做 adversarial paraphrasing）\n4. Cross-encoder 验证 — RoBERTa-base 重复核心实验（9-class + MLM + vanilla），证明不是 DeBERTa-specific\n5. LLM-as-judge OOD（DD/FITD）\n\n**请你从以下维度评估**：\n1. 哪些实验对审稿人最有说服力？按提分潜力排序\n2. 有没有我遗漏的高价值实验方向？\n3. 哪些实验可以组合/复用 GPU 提高效率？\n4. 每个实验的风险评估：结果不好怎么办？能否正面融入叙事？\n5. 如果只能做 3 个，选哪 3 个？如果全做，优先级排序？\n6. 对论文 framing 的影响：哪些实验成功后可以升级 claim？\n\n不要保守，目标是 main conference accept。"

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] MF2 统计检验结果审查 — 两个方法学问题

## 结果概要

McNemar's test (DD OOD) + Bootstrap 95% CI 已完成。关键发现：scrambled vs binary K=2 p=0.0412 (显著)；其余对比不显著。

## 问题 1: n=118 来源不明 [warning]

Worker 报告 "DD OOD, n=118"，但论文 DD OOD 数据集为 80 条对话。118 的来源需要确认：
- 是否混入了 FITD 数据 (80+38)?
- 是否按 turn 而非 conversation 计数?
- 如果 n 不正确，所有 p 值和 CI 无效。

**建议**: 核实评估脚本的数据加载逻辑。

## 问题 2: Seed 不匹配导致不公平对比 [warning]

Worker 注明 "binary 使用的是 mf1_binary_seed456 (seed=456)，因为 plan_013_binary (seed=42) 的 checkpoint 已被删除。"

| Variant | Seed used | DD K=2 F1 |
|---------|-----------|-----------|
| 9-class | seed 42 (presumably) | 0.981 |
| Scrambled | seed 42 | 0.944* |
| Binary | **seed 456** | **1.000** |

用不同 seed 做 McNemar 对比（scrambled seed42 vs binary seed456）无意义——seed 差异可能大于 variant 差异。这解释了为什么 binary 在 CI 中显示 [1.0, 1.0] 而 scrambled 显示 [0.925, 0.988]——这反映的是 seed 差异，不是 variant 差异。

**p=0.0412 (scrambled vs binary K=2) 不可信**，因为对比的不是同一 seed。

## 建议

1. 用 MF1 完成后的 matched-seed 数据重跑：scrambled seed 456 vs binary seed 456, scrambled seed 123 vs binary seed 123
2. 或 aggregate across seeds: 对每个 seed 跑 McNemar，然后报告一致性
3. n=118 必须解释清楚再使用
4. 论文中仅纳入方法学正确的统计检验结果

**对论文影响**: 当前 MF2 结果不宜直接写入论文。需要修正后重跑。但 McNemar / bootstrap 方法本身正确，修正 seed 匹配 + 验证 n 后可用。

  "content": "[Reviewer 回复]:\n[severity: info] MF1 multi-seed 全部完成 — 汇总审查\n\n## 完整 3-seed 结果\n\n**Binary (DD Full F1)**:\n- Seed 42: 0.990 | Seed 123: 1.000 | Seed 456: 1.000\n- **Mean: 0.997, Pop std: 0.005**\n\n**Scrambled (DD Full F1)**:\n- Seed 42: 0.990 | Seed 123: 1.000 | Seed 456: 1.000\n- **Mean: 0.997, Pop std: 0.005**\n\n**对照 9-class**: Mean 0.997, Pop std 0.005\n\n## 关键发现\n\n三个分类监督 variant 的 3-seed 表现**完全一致**: scrambled (0.997±0.005) = binary (0.997±0.005) = 9-class (0.997±0.005)\n\n这**强化**了论文核心论证：\n1. 分类 head 是 regularizer，label 的正确性和粒度对 **detection** 无影响\n2. 所有分类监督 variant 的 cross-seed variance 都极低（std=0.005），与 MLM (0.115) 形成鲜明对比\n3. Evidence hierarchy 中 \"scrambled (0.99) ≈ binary (0.99)\" 可更新为精确的 \"0.997±0.005\"\n\n## 论文更新建议\n\n1. **Table 2** (evidence_hierarchy): binary 和 scrambled 更新为 .997±.005（从单 seed .990 → 3-seed mean）\n2. **Metrics 段**: \"binary and scrambled use seed 42\" → \"binary and scrambled report 3-seed mean±std (seeds 42, 123, 456)\"\n3. **Evidence hierarchy 文字**: \"scrambled (0.99) ≈ binary (0.99) ≈ 9-class (0.997±0.005)\" → \"scrambled (0.997±0.005) = binary (0.997±0.005) = 9-class (0.997±0.005)\"\n4. **Limitations**: 删除 \"Binary, scrambled...report single-seed evaluations\" 这一条（mean pooling 仍然是单 seed，可单独保留）\n5. **Discussion 可增强论点**: 三种分类监督 variant 的完全一致进一步确认分类 head 是 representational regularizer\n\n## Cross-seed 稳定性验证 ✅\n\n三个 variant 都呈现相同的 seed pattern: seed 42 = 0.990, seed 123 = 1.000, seed 456 = 1.000。这种一致性说明 seed 间差异来自 Stage-2 GRU 初始化，而非 Stage-1 encoder 差异——与论文 \"classification supervision provides stability\" 的 claim 一致。"

_source line 3611 · 2026-05-18T09:42:11.322Z_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: info] MF1 multi-seed 全部完成 — 汇总审查

## 完整 3-seed 结果

**Binary (DD Full F1)**:
- Seed 42: 0.990 | Seed 123: 1.000 | Seed 456: 1.000
- **Mean: 0.997, Pop std: 0.005**

**Scrambled (DD Full F1)**:
- Seed 42: 0.990 | Seed 123: 1.000 | Seed 456: 1.000
- **Mean: 0.997, Pop std: 0.005**

**对照 9-class**: Mean 0.997, Pop std 0.005

## 关键发现

三个分类监督 variant 的 3-seed 表现**完全一致**: scrambled (0.997±0.005) = binary (0.997±0.005) = 9-class (0.997±0.005)

这**强化**了论文核心论证：
1. 分类 head 是 regularizer，label 的正确性和粒度对 **detection** 无影响
2. 所有分类监督 variant 的 cross-seed variance 都极低（std=0.005），与 MLM (0.115) 形成鲜明对比
3. Evidence hierarchy 中 "scrambled (0.99) ≈ binary (0.99)" 可更新为精确的 "0.997±0.005"

## 论文更新建议

1. **Table 2** (evidence_hierarchy): binary 和 scrambled 更新为 .997±.005（从单 seed .990 → 3-seed mean）
2. **Metrics 段**: "binary and scrambled use seed 42" → "binary and scrambled report 3-seed mean±std (seeds 42, 123, 456)"
3. **Evidence hierarchy 文字**: "scrambled (0.99) ≈ binary (0.99) ≈ 9-class (0.997±0.005)" → "scrambled (0.997±0.005) = binary (0.997±0.005) = 9-class (0.997±0.005)"
4. **Limitations**: 删除 "Binary, scrambled...report single-seed evaluations" 这一条（mean pooling 仍然是单 seed，可单独保留）
5. **Discussion 可增强论点**: 三种分类监督 variant 的完全一致进一步确认分类 head 是 representational regularizer

## Cross-seed 稳定性验证 ✅

三个 variant 都呈现相同的 seed pattern: seed 42 = 0.990, seed 123 = 1.000, seed 456 = 1.000。这种一致性说明 seed 间差异来自 Stage-2 GRU 初始化，而非 Stage-1 encoder 差异——与论文 "classification supervision provides stability" 的 claim 一致。

````json
{
  "type": "queue-operation",
  "operation": "enqueue",
  "timestamp": "2026-05-18T09:44:09.866Z",
  "sessionId": "c18fb2ef-d798-44ee-947c-99044ca76999",
  "content": "[Reviewer 回复]:\n[severity: info] Weak Reject → Accept 路径独立评估\n\n## 一、提分潜力排序（6→8/10 目标）\n\n### Tier 1: 必须做，每个 +0.5-1.0 分\n\n**1. Cross-encoder 验证 (RoBERTa-base)** ⭐ 最高价值\n- 做什么：RoBERTa-base 重复 9-class + MLM + vanilla → DD OOD 评估\n- 为什么：证明 findings 是 methodology-general 而非 DeBERTa-specific。这是审稿人最容易提出的攻击面：\"你的结论可能只是 DeBERTa 的特性\"\n- GPU 成本：3 × 30min Stage-1 = 1.5h + Stage-2 < 15min\n- **成功后**：abstract 可以去掉 \"DeBERTa-based\" 限定词，claim 升级为 \"encoder-agnostic continued pretraining is the primary mechanism\"\n- 风险评估：低。核心发现是 continued pretraining 的效果（通用原理，gururangan2020dontstop 已在多 encoder 验证）。如果 RoBERTa 结果不一致，可以作为 \"encoder choice matters\" 的发现融入分析\n\n**2. MF5 ActorAttack OOD (80条)** ⭐ 必做\n- 做什么：Qwen3-8B 生成 80 条 ActorAttack 风格多轮对话，所有 7 variant 评估\n- 为什么：当前只有 DD 是有效 OOD（FITD 太容易，vanilla 都 0.99）。第三个有效 OOD 数据集从根本上解决 \"single effective OOD dataset\" 问题\n- GPU 成本：生成 ~2h + 评估 7 variant < 1h\n- **成功后**：claim 从 \"three attack families (one effective OOD)\" 升级为 \"three effective OOD datasets\"\n- 风险评估：中。如果 ActorAttack 的 multi-persona steering 结构与 Crescendo 太相似，可能像 FITD 一样太容易。如果太难（所有 variant 都失败），说明训练数据多样性不足——可诚实讨论。任何结果都能融入 evidence hierarchy 分析\n\n**3. Probing experiments** ⭐ 直接回应 R1-W4\n- 做什么：\n  (a) Linear probing on [CLS] embeddings: 能否预测 turn position（1st vs later）、escalation level（early vs late-stage）、persuasion presence (yes/no)\n  (b) CKA/RSA 比较 vanilla vs MLM vs 9-class 的表征结构\n  (c) Token-level attention 分析：discourse markers vs content words\n- 为什么：TF-IDF 0.944 是审稿人最容易的攻击点 \"你的模型可能只是在做词袋匹配\"。Probing 可以直接证明 [CLS] 编码了 discourse structure\n- GPU 成本：< 1h（只需 forward pass + linear classifiers）\n- **成功后**：Limitations 中 \"We cannot conclusively distinguish...\" 可以升级为 \"Probing confirms discourse-level structure\"\n- 风险评估：中。如果 probing 显示主要是 lexical features → 论文已有 limitation 讨论，可强化为 \"lexical features sufficient for detection, discourse features contribute to robustness\"。不致命\n\n### Tier 2: 有余力做，每个 +0.2-0.5 分\n\n**4. DD OOD perturbation 扩展 (n=76→156)**\n- 在 80 条 DD OOD 上做 adversarial paraphrasing → 所有 variant 评估\n- 解决 \"n=76 too small for three-layer attribution\" 问题\n- GPU 成本：< 2h（paraphrase generation + evaluation）\n- 风险：低。Full-length 0pp 对 classification variants 应该稳定\n\n**5. LLM-as-judge OOD**\n- GPT-5.5 / Qwen3-8B 在 DD + FITD 上做 zero-shot detection\n- 补全 baseline 对比（当前只有 IID 结果）\n- 成本：API 费用，非 GPU\n- 风险：极低（IID 已经很差，OOD 不太可能更好）\n\n## 二、我补充的遗漏方向\n\n**6. 生成器泛化性测试** — 被忽视的高价值方向\n- 用 Llama-3-70B 或 GPT-4o 生成一批 DD 对话（40-80条），作为 \"generator-transfer\" OOD 测试\n- 当前所有数据来自 Qwen3-8B，审稿人可质疑 \"可能只是学了 Qwen 的写作风格\"\n- 如果检测性能保持 → \"generalization beyond generator\" 是强 claim\n- 成本：API 费用 + 评估时间 < 2h\n\n## 三、GPU 并行方案\n\n```\nDay 1 (8h window):\n  GPU 0: RoBERTa 9-class Stage-1 (30min) → Stage-2 (5min) → DD eval\n  GPU 1: RoBERTa MLM Stage-1 (30min) → Stage-2 (5min) → DD eval\n  GPU 2: ActorAttack 数据生成 (2-3h) → 7 variant Stage-2 + eval\n  GPU 3: Probing experiments (forward pass + linear probes, 1-2h)\n\nDay 1 evening:\n  All GPUs: DD perturbation paraphrase generation + evaluation\n  API parallel: LLM-as-judge OOD + generator-transfer data generation\n```\n\nTotal GPU: < 20h on 4 GPUs = < 80 GPU·h（预算 672h 的 12%）\n\n## 四、风险矩阵\n\n| 实验 | 好结果概率 | 坏结果融入叙事 |\n|------|----------|--------------|\n| RoBERTa | 80% | \"encoder choice is a factor\" — 增加一个分析维度 |\n| ActorAttack | 70% | \"training data diversity matters\" — 明确 future work |\n| Probing | 60% | \"lexical for detection, discourse for robustness\" — 已有框架 |\n| DD perturbation | 90% | 几乎不可能坏 |\n| LLM-judge OOD | 95% | 几乎不可能坏 |\n| Generator transfer | 65% | \"generator-specific features\" — interesting finding |\n\n**任何一个实验的\"坏\"结果都不会使论文变差**，只是无法升级 claim。论文当前的 \"preliminary findings\" 基调已经足够防御。\n\n## 五、Top 3 选择\n\n**如果只做 3 个: Cross-encoder + ActorAttack + Probing**\n\n理由：\n1. Cross-encoder → 消除 \"encoder-specific\" 攻击面 → methodology contribution 升级\n2. ActorAttack → 消除 \"single OOD\" 攻击面 → evaluation robustness 升级\n3. Probing → 消除 \"just lexical\" 攻击面 → mechanism understanding 升级\n\n这三个分别回答了审稿人最可能的三个攻击：\"只在一个 encoder 上试过吗？\"\"只在一个 OOD 上试过吗？\"\"是不是只是词袋匹配？\"\n\n## 六、Claim 升级路径\n\n**当前 (Weak Reject)**: \"preliminary findings on synthetic conversations from three attack families using a DeBERTa-based framework\"\n\n**Top 3 全成功后**: \"systematic evidence that continued pretraining is an encoder-agnostic mechanism for cross-attack generalization, validated across two encoder architectures, three OOD attack families, with confirmed discourse-level representational features beyond lexical cues\"\n\n**全部 6 个成功后**: 上述 + \"robust to generator transfer\" + \"statistically validated robustness attribution\"\n\n从 \"preliminary\" 到 \"systematic evidence\" 的转变足以从 Weak Reject (2/5) 翻到 Weak Accept (4/5)。"
}

## 一、提分潜力排序（6→8/10 目标）

### Tier 1: 必须做，每个 +0.5-1.0 分

**1. Cross-encoder 验证 (RoBERTa-base)** ⭐ 最高价值
- 做什么：RoBERTa-base 重复 9-class + MLM + vanilla → DD OOD 评估
- 为什么：证明 findings 是 methodology-general 而非 DeBERTa-specific。这是审稿人最容易提出的攻击面："你的结论可能只是 DeBERTa 的特性"
- GPU 成本：3 × 30min Stage-1 = 1.5h + Stage-2 < 15min
- **成功后**：abstract 可以去掉 "DeBERTa-based" 限定词，claim 升级为 "encoder-agnostic continued pretraining is the primary mechanism"
- 风险评估：低。核心发现是 continued pretraining 的效果（通用原理，gururangan2020dontstop 已在多 encoder 验证）。如果 RoBERTa 结果不一致，可以作为 "encoder choice matters" 的发现融入分析

**2. MF5 ActorAttack OOD (80条)** ⭐ 必做
- 做什么：Qwen3-8B 生成 80 条 ActorAttack 风格多轮对话，所有 7 variant 评估
- 为什么：当前只有 DD 是有效 OOD（FITD 太容易，vanilla 都 0.99）。第三个有效 OOD 数据集从根本上解决 "single effective OOD dataset" 问题
- GPU 成本：生成 ~2h + 评估 7 variant < 1h
- **成功后**：claim 从 "three attack families (one effective OOD)" 升级为 "three effective OOD datasets"
- 风险评估：中。如果 ActorAttack 的 multi-persona steering 结构与 Crescendo 太相似，可能像 FITD 一样太容易。如果太难（所有 variant 都失败），说明训练数据多样性不足——可诚实讨论。任何结果都能融入 evidence hierarchy 分析

**3. Probing experiments** ⭐ 直接回应 R1-W4
- 做什么：
  (a) Linear probing on [CLS] embeddings: 能否预测 turn position（1st vs later）、escalation level（early vs late-stage）、persuasion presence (yes/no)
  (b) CKA/RSA 比较 vanilla vs MLM vs 9-class 的表征结构
  (c) Token-level attention 分析：discourse markers vs content words
- 为什么：TF-IDF 0.944 是审稿人最容易的攻击点 "你的模型可能只是在做词袋匹配"。Probing 可以直接证明 [CLS] 编码了 discourse structure
- GPU 成本：< 1h（只需 forward pass + linear classifiers）
- **成功后**：Limitations 中 "We cannot conclusively distinguish..." 可以升级为 "Probing confirms discourse-level structure"
- 风险评估：中。如果 probing 显示主要是 lexical features → 论文已有 limitation 讨论，可强化为 "lexical features sufficient for detection, discourse features contribute to robustness"。不致命

### Tier 2: 有余力做，每个 +0.2-0.5 分

**4. DD OOD perturbation 扩展 (n=76→156)**
- 在 80 条 DD OOD 上做 adversarial paraphrasing → 所有 variant 评估
- 解决 "n=76 too small for three-layer attribution" 问题
- GPU 成本：< 2h（paraphrase generation + evaluation）
- 风险：低。Full-length 0pp 对 classification variants 应该稳定

**5. LLM-as-judge OOD**
- GPT-5.5 / Qwen3-8B 在 DD + FITD 上做 zero-shot detection
- 补全 baseline 对比（当前只有 IID 结果）
- 成本：API 费用，非 GPU
- 风险：极低（IID 已经很差，OOD 不太可能更好）

## 二、我补充的遗漏方向

**6. 生成器泛化性测试** — 被忽视的高价值方向
- 用 Llama-3-70B 或 GPT-4o 生成一批 DD 对话（40-80条），作为 "generator-transfer" OOD 测试
- 当前所有数据来自 Qwen3-8B，审稿人可质疑 "可能只是学了 Qwen 的写作风格"
- 如果检测性能保持 → "generalization beyond generator" 是强 claim
- 成本：API 费用 + 评估时间 < 2h

## 三、GPU 并行方案

```
Day 1 (8h window):
  GPU 0: RoBERTa 9-class Stage-1 (30min) → Stage-2 (5min) → DD eval
  GPU 1: RoBERTa MLM Stage-1 (30min) → Stage-2 (5min) → DD eval
  GPU 2: ActorAttack 数据生成 (2-3h) → 7 variant Stage-2 + eval
  GPU 3: Probing experiments (forward pass + linear probes, 1-2h)

Day 1 evening:
  All GPUs: DD perturbation paraphrase generation + evaluation
  API parallel: LLM-as-judge OOD + generator-transfer data generation
```

Total GPU: < 20h on 4 GPUs = < 80 GPU·h（预算 672h 的 12%）

## 四、风险矩阵

| 实验 | 好结果概率 | 坏结果融入叙事 |
|------|----------|--------------|
| RoBERTa | 80% | "encoder choice is a factor" — 增加一个分析维度 |
| ActorAttack | 70% | "training data diversity matters" — 明确 future work |
| Probing | 60% | "lexical for detection, discourse for robustness" — 已有框架 |
| DD perturbation | 90% | 几乎不可能坏 |
| LLM-judge OOD | 95% | 几乎不可能坏 |
| Generator transfer | 65% | "generator-specific features" — interesting finding |

**任何一个实验的"坏"结果都不会使论文变差**，只是无法升级 claim。论文当前的 "preliminary findings" 基调已经足够防御。

## 五、Top 3 选择

**如果只做 3 个: Cross-encoder + ActorAttack + Probing**

理由：
1. Cross-encoder → 消除 "encoder-specific" 攻击面 → methodology contribution 升级
2. ActorAttack → 消除 "single OOD" 攻击面 → evaluation robustness 升级
3. Probing → 消除 "just lexical" 攻击面 → mechanism understanding 升级

这三个分别回答了审稿人最可能的三个攻击："只在一个 encoder 上试过吗？""只在一个 OOD 上试过吗？""是不是只是词袋匹配？"

## 六、Claim 升级路径

**当前 (Weak Reject)**: "preliminary findings on synthetic conversations from three attack families using a DeBERTa-based framework"

**Top 3 全成功后**: "systematic evidence that continued pretraining is an encoder-agnostic mechanism for cross-attack generalization, validated across two encoder architectures, three OOD attack families, with confirmed discourse-level representational features beyond lexical cues"

**全部 6 个成功后**: 上述 + "robust to generator transfer" + "statistically validated robustness attribution"

从 "preliminary" 到 "systematic evidence" 的转变足以从 Weak Reject (2/5) 翻到 Weak Accept (4/5)。

{
  "project": "persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect",
  "content": "# persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect — Research Dashboard\n\n## 核心方向\n系统性分解多轮越狱检测的跨攻击泛化机制。核心发现：domain-adaptive continued pretraining 是主要机制（vanilla 0.28→MLM 0.91±0.12），分类监督提供稳定性（0.997±0.005）、鲁棒性（0pp perturbation）和可解释性（IID scope）。详见 paper_framing.md。\n\n## 当前阶段\n**扩展实验阶段 — 目标 Weak Reject → Accept — DDL 2026-05-25**\n\n审稿修复 MF1/SF9/MF2 完成。论文写作修复全部完成（SF1-SF12 resolved）。\nMF1 关键发现：scrambled = binary = 9-class = 0.997±0.005，regularizer 论证彻底站住。\n\n现启动 6 个扩展实验，目标从 \"preliminary findings on DeBERTa\" 升级到 \"systematic encoder-agnostic evidence\"。\n\n### 扩展实验状态（Tier 1: 每个 +0.5-1.0 分）\n- [ ] EXP-1: Cross-encoder (RoBERTa-base) 9-class + MLM + vanilla — Agent 已派, cuda:0+1\n- [ ] EXP-2: ActorAttack OOD 80条生成 + 7 variant 评估 — Agent 已派, cuda:2\n- [ ] EXP-3: Probing experiments (linear probe + CKA) — Agent 已派, cuda:3\n\n### 扩展实验状态（Tier 2: 每个 +0.2-0.5 分）\n- [ ] EXP-4: DD perturbation 扩展 n=76→156 — 等 Tier 1 GPU 释放\n- [ ] EXP-5: LLM-as-judge OOD (Qwen3-8B) — CPU/API\n- [ ] EXP-6: Generator transfer test — 需 API\n\n### 审稿 Must-Fix 状态\n- [x] MF1: binary/scrambled 多 seed → 0.997±0.005 = 9-class ✅\n- [ ] MF2: 统计检验 → n=118 问题 + seed 不匹配, 需重跑 (等 EXP-4)\n- [x] MF3: DeepContext 重构 ✅\n- [x] MF4: claim 降级 ✅\n- [ ] MF5/EXP-2: ActorAttack OOD → 进行中\n\n### Claim 升级路径\n当前: \"preliminary findings on synthetic conversations using DeBERTa-based framework\"\nTop 3 成功后: \"systematic evidence that continued pretraining is an encoder-agnostic mechanism, validated across two architectures, three OOD families, with confirmed discourse-level features\"\n\n## GPU 预算\nwestd-33183: 4× RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell\n扩展实验总成本: ~80 GPU·h（预算 672h 的 12%）\n当前: 4 GPU 全分配给 EXP-1/2/3\n\n## 关键风险\n1. RoBERTa 如果不复现 evidence hierarchy → 降级为 \"encoder choice matters\" 分析维度\n2. ActorAttack 可能太容易（像 FITD）或太难 → 任何结果可融入叙事\n3. Probing 如果只找到 lexical features → \"lexical for detection, discourse for robustness\"\n",

}

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] EXP-1 RoBERTa 9-class 审查 — 结果可信但有关键缺口

## 数据核实
Worker 汇总与服务器 `results/exp1_roberta_9class.json` 完全一致。✓
训练代码使用 `/root/autodl-tmp/roberta-base-manual`（RobertaModel LOAD REPORT 确认），超参与 DeBERTa 对齐（lr=2e-5, batch=16, epochs=4, alpha=0.3, max_len=256）。✓
使用 cuda:0，在白名单内。✓

## 结果对比（RoBERTa 9-class vs DeBERTa 9-class）

| K | RoBERTa (3-seed) | DeBERTa (3-seed) | Gap |
|---|-----------------|-----------------|-----|
| 1 | 0.987±0.012 | 1.000±0.000 | -1.3pp |
| 2 | 0.950±0.028 | ~0.997±0.005 | **-4.7pp** |
| 3 | 0.956±0.019 | ~0.997±0.005 | -4.1pp |
| 5 | 0.987±0.009 | 1.000±0.000 | -1.3pp |
| Full | 0.994±0.005 | 0.997±0.005 | -0.3pp |

Persuasion classification: RoBERTa 0.84 vs DeBERTa 1.0

## 审查发现

1. [severity: info] **Full-length 检测近乎等价**（0.994 vs 0.997），支持 encoder-agnostic claim
2. [severity: info] **Despite 更差的策略分类（0.84 vs 1.0），检测仍然 work** → 进一步支持 regularizer hypothesis：不需要"学好"策略，分类任务本身就够
3. [severity: warning] **Early detection (K=2) 退化明显**：4.7pp gap + 5.6x 更大 variance（0.028 vs 0.005）。论文必须诚实报告此差异，不能 claim "encoder-agnostic early detection"
4. [severity: warning] **Evidence hierarchy 缺 MLM + vanilla RoBERTa**：当前只有 9-class。没有 RoBERTa vanilla 和 RoBERTa MLM，无法验证 hierarchy 是否 replicate（vanilla 差→MLM 中→9-class 强）。这是 "encoder-agnostic evidence hierarchy" claim 的关键缺口
5. [severity: warning] **Registry 无 EXP-1 条目** — 实验未注册，违反可追溯性要求
6. [severity: info] **GPUs 再次全部空闲** — EXP-2 (ActorAttack) 和 EXP-3 (Probing) 仍无代码/进程/registry 迹象

## 结论
RoBERTa 9-class 结果真实可信，Full F1 supports encoder-agnostic claim。但 claim 范围需要约束（early detection 有 gap），且 evidence hierarchy 完整复制需要 MLM + vanilla variants。建议尽快启动 RoBERTa MLM + vanilla 实验。

{
  "project": "persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect",
  "message": "⚠️ GPU 空闲警报 — 4 GPU 全空，EXP-2/3 未启动。立即行动。\n\n处理完当前 worker 回调后，**立刻并行启动以下 4 个任务，不要串行等**：\n\n## 立即启动（4 GPU 全占满）\n\n**cuda:0 — RoBERTa MLM-only**\n用 roberta-base 做 MLM-only Stage-1 (无 classification head)，同 DeBERTa MLM 的 config。Stage-2 BiGRU + DD OOD eval。3 seeds (42, 123, 456)。\n\n**cuda:1 — RoBERTa vanilla**\n不训练 Stage-1，直接用 off-the-shelf roberta-base 做 Stage-2 BiGRU + DD OOD eval。3 seeds (42, 123, 456)。这个很快（只有 Stage-2）。完成后 cuda:1 立即启动 EXP-3 Probing。\n\n**cuda:2 — EXP-2 ActorAttack OOD 数据生成**\n用 Qwen3-8B 生成 80 条 ActorAttack 风格对话。参考 ActorAttack 论文 (ren2024actorattack) 的 multi-persona steering 结构。生成后做 9-class strategy annotation + 所有 7 DeBERTa variant 评估。\n\n**cuda:3 — EXP-3 Probing experiments**\n用已有 DeBERTa checkpoints (seed=42) 提取 [CLS] embeddings，做 linear probing (turn position / escalation / persuasion presence) + CKA 分析 (vanilla vs MLM vs 9-class)。\n\n**每个实验必须注册到 registry**（之前 EXP-1 缺 registry 条目，不可重复）。\n\n时间紧迫，DDL 7 天，GPU 不能空。"
}

_source line 3740 · 2026-05-18T10:43:30.353Z_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: critical] EXP-1 RoBERTa MLM + Vanilla 审查 — Evidence Hierarchy 未在 RoBERTa 上复现，Claim 升级路径受阻

## 数据核实
服务器 `results/exp1_roberta_mlm.json` 和 `exp1_roberta_vanilla.json` 与 worker 汇总一致。✓
代码核实：vanilla 使用 off-the-shelf RoBERTa frozen [CLS] → GRU，与 DeBERTa vanilla 实验方法完全对齐。✓

## 完整 RoBERTa vs DeBERTa Evidence Hierarchy（DD OOD Full F1）

| Variant | DeBERTa | RoBERTa | 差异 |
|---------|---------|---------|------|
| Vanilla | **0.28** (seed42) | **0.981±0.026** | **+70pp** |
| MLM | 0.91±0.12 | 0.987±0.018 | +7.7pp |
| 9-class | 0.997±0.005 | 0.994±0.005 | -0.3pp |

DeBERTa hierarchy: 0.28 → 0.91 → 0.997 (巨大梯度，续训=主要机制)
RoBERTa hierarchy: 0.981 → 0.987 → 0.994 (**本质平坦，续训边际贡献**)

## 核心问题

1. **"Continued pretraining is the primary mechanism" 在 RoBERTa 上不成立**。RoBERTa vanilla 已达 0.981，续训只加 0.6pp（MLM）或 1.3pp（9-class）。62pp gap 是 DeBERTa 特有现象。

2. **原因推测**：DeBERTa-v3 使用 replaced token detection (RTD) 预训练，其 [CLS] 表征质量不经 fine-tuning 极差。RoBERTa 使用标准 MLM 预训练，[CLS] off-the-shelf 即可用。这不是"domain adaptation"效果，而是"修复 DeBERTa [CLS]"效果。

3. **Claim 升级路径受阻**：计划的 "preliminary findings on DeBERTa" → "systematic encoder-agnostic evidence" 不可行。

## 仍然成立的发现

- **Variance reduction replicates**: RoBERTa vanilla std=0.026-0.134 → 9-class std=0.005-0.028（尤其 K=2: 0.134→0.028），分类监督降方差是跨编码器的
- **Full-length 检测**：两个编码器都可达 F1>0.99（不同路径）
- **Regularizer hypothesis 加强**：RoBERTa 策略分类 acc=0.84（远低于 DeBERTa 的 1.0），但检测 F1 相当 → 分类不需要做好，作为 regularizer 就够

## 建议论文方向调整

不能 claim "encoder-agnostic mechanism"，但可 claim：
1. "对 off-the-shelf 表征差的编码器（如 DeBERTa-v3），continued pretraining 是关键"
2. "跨编码器一致：分类监督显著降低方差" 
3. "编码器选择影响是否需要续训，但两条路径都可达 >0.99 检测"
4. RoBERTa 实验实际上增加了 paper 的深度和诚实度（比只报 DeBERTa 更强）

这是一个 negative result for the planned upgrade，但如果 framed correctly，是论文的**正面补充**。

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
This is a critical finding. The RoBERTa evidence hierarchy is essentially flat (0.981 → 0.987 → 0.994), meaning the "continued pretraining is the primary mechanism" claim is DeBERTa-specific, not encoder-agnostic. The 62pp gap was because DeBERTa-v3's RTD pretraining produces poor [CLS] representations without fine-tuning.

This is a significant negative result for the planned claim upgrade. But the Reviewer is right that it can be framed positively:


Variance reduction through classification supervision generalizes across both encoders, and since both reach >0.99 detection accuracy, I can reframe this as showing that encoder choice determines the baseline performance gap rather than the effectiveness of the approach itself. This actually strengthens the paper's honesty by revealing that the DeBERTa improvement is about fixing poor [CLS] representations specifically, not just domain adaptation. I need to adjust the framing in the paper—can't claim encoder-agnostic results from preliminary findings, but I can confidently claim the variance reduction mechanism works cross-encoder and both paths converge to high performance. whether the pretrained [CLS] representations are already suitable for the task. DeBERTa-v3 needs domain adaptation because its RTD pretraining leaves it less equipped for this specific problem, while RoBERTa's MLM pretraining gets it most of the way there. Both converge to >0.99 accuracy with proper training, and classification supervision consistently reduces variance across runs.

This actually strengthens the contribution—it's not just that continued pretraining works universally, but that the *need* for it depends on the encoder's pretraining method. So the core claim should be reframed: the primary mechanism isn't continued pretraining itself, but rather whether the encoder's off-the-shelf representations are already well-suited to the task.
```

```

{
  "text": "EXP-1 Cross-encoder 结果出来了，有重要发现：\n\n**RoBERTa vanilla 直接达到 F1=0.981**，续训仅加 1.3pp（DeBERTa 是 62pp gap）。原因：DeBERTa-v3 用 replaced token detection 预训练，off-the-shelf [CLS] 质量差；RoBERTa 用标准 MLM，[CLS] 已经好用。\n\n**这意味着**：\n- ❌ 不能 claim \"encoder-agnostic: continued pretraining is universally the primary mechanism\"\n- ✅ 但揭示了更深的洞察：**续训的必要性取决于编码器预训练方式**\n- ✅ 跨编码器一致：分类监督显著降 variance（DeBERTa 0.115→0.005, RoBERTa 0.026→0.005）\n- ✅ 两个编码器都达 F1>0.99，regularizer hypothesis 加强\n\n论文 framing 正在调整。这实际上使论文更有深度——比简单的 \"encoder-agnostic\" 更有 mechanistic insight，审稿人会更喜欢。\n\nEXP-2 (ActorAttack) 和 EXP-3 (Probing) 已催促 Agent 立即启动。"
}

16	Our contributions on this preliminary study of synthetic multi-turn jailbreak conversations:
17	\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*, itemsep=2pt, topsep=4pt]
18	  \item \textbf{Systematic decomposition.} Seven controlled DeBERTa-v3 variants identify continued pretraining as the primary mechanism for cross-attack generalization on this encoder ($\sim$62\,pp OOD gap bridged by MLM alone). Misaligned supervision (topic classification) actively destabilizes representations.
19	
20	  \item \textbf{Cross-encoder validation.} RoBERTa achieves F1\,=\,0.981$\pm$0.026 without domain adaptation; the necessity of continued pretraining depends on encoder pretraining method. Classification supervision reduces variance to near-zero in both architectures (Section~\ref{sec:cross_encoder}).
21	
22	  \item \textbf{Complementary role of classification supervision.} Classification adds perturbation robustness (zero degradation at full length on DeBERTa, $n$=76) and interpretable characterization of known attack families, though strategy predictions do not transfer to unseen attacks (Section~\ref{sec:strategy}).
23	
24	  \item \textbf{Three-layer robustness attribution.} Under adversarial paraphrasing on DeBERTa, classification structure suffices at full length; correct and fine-grained labels are jointly required at $K$=2; both properties contribute independently at $K$=1.
25	\end{itemize}
26	
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_014uPsTryXefBN1YDyjZL82V`</summary>

```text
1	% abstract.tex — cross-encoder revision
2	\begin{abstract}
3	Multi-turn jailbreak attacks distribute adversarial intent across seemingly benign conversational turns, evading per-turn safety filters.
4	We present preliminary findings from a systematic ablation on two encoder architectures that decomposes what enables cross-attack generalization in multi-turn jailbreak detection.
5	On DeBERTa-v3, continued pretraining is the primary mechanism: masked language modeling without labels bridges $\sim$62 percentage points (pp) of the out-of-distribution (OOD) gap (vanilla F1\,=\,0.28 $\to$ 0.91$\pm$0.12 on unseen attack types).
6	Cross-encoder validation on RoBERTa reveals that this effect is encoder-dependent: RoBERTa achieves F1\,=\,0.981$\pm$0.026 without domain adaptation. This indicates that DeBERTa-v3's replaced token detection pretraining produces representations that require domain-specific repair, while standard MLM pretraining yields usable representations without further training.
7	Across both encoders, classification supervision reduces cross-seed variance to near-zero (std 0.005) and provides perturbation robustness on DeBERTa (zero degradation at full conversation length vs.\ $\sim$12\,pp for non-adapted models, $n$=76).
8	These results on synthetic conversations from three attack families suggest that encoder pretraining method determines the necessity of domain adaptation for multi-turn jailbreak detection; validation on larger, human-generated datasets is needed.
9	\end{abstract}
10	
```

181	\midrule
182	Wikipedia MLM & $-$12.1 & $-$12.4 & $-$15.4 & $-$17.9 \\
183	Vanilla DeBERTa & $-$11.8 & $-$14.6 & $-$12.1 & $-$13.8 \\
184	\bottomrule
185	\end{tabular}
186	\end{table}
187	
188	Table~\ref{tab:perturbation} reveals a three-layer robustness attribution.
189	All classification-supervised variants (9-class, binary, scrambled) achieve zero degradation at full conversation length and $K \geq 3$, regardless of label correctness.
190	Classification training structure alone suffices for full-length robustness.
191	At $K$=2, the combination of correct and fine-grained labels is required: only 9-class ($\Delta$=0.0) maintains zero degradation, while scrambled (random labels, fine-grained) and binary (correct labels, coarse) both degrade by $-$2.6\,pp.
192	The fact that scrambled = binary at $K$=2 shows that neither granularity alone nor correctness alone suffices; the 9-class advantage requires both properties jointly.
193	At $K$=1, both granularity and correctness contribute independently: granularity provides +3.9\,pp (scrambled $-$5.3 vs.\ binary $-$9.2\,pp), and correctness provides +2.7\,pp (9-class $-$2.6 vs.\ scrambled $-$5.3\,pp).
194	Correct fine-grained labels yield the most perturbation-invariant early-turn representations.
195	
196	Jailbreak-domain models without classification (MLM, topic, TF-IDF) degrade by 1--4\,pp at full length.
197	Models without jailbreak-domain exposure (Wikipedia MLM, vanilla) degrade by $\sim$12\,pp, despite Wikipedia MLM matching jailbreak MLM on detection (Section~\ref{sec:decomposition}). Perturbation robustness requires jailbreak-domain exposure specifically.
198	McNemar's test on matched-seed predictions confirms that no pairwise differences among classification-supervised variants (9-class vs.\ binary, 9-class vs.\ scrambled, scrambled vs.\ binary) reach statistical significance at any prefix length (all $p > 0.13$).
199	Discordant pairs are sparse ($\leq$7 per comparison), and the direction of advantage is inconsistent across seeds (e.g., 9-class outperforms scrambled at seed 42 but the reverse holds at seed 456 for $K$=1).
200	The perturbation evaluation ($n$=76 IID, with discordant pairs $\leq$7) has limited statistical power to detect differences of this magnitude; the three-layer structure should be interpreted as an observed pattern rather than a statistically confirmed hierarchy.
201	
202	%----------------------------------------------------------------------
203	\subsection{Strategy Trajectory Analysis}
204	\label{sec:strategy}
205	
206	The 9-class persuasion taxonomy provides interpretable characterization of attack strategies on known attack families.
207	The predicted strategy labels achieve Cohen's $\kappa$=0.78 agreement with human annotations on IID data ($n$=50; see Section~\ref{sec:method}). This enables post-hoc analysis of \emph{how} different attack families orchestrate persuasion.
208	
209	\begin{figure*}[t]
210	\centering
211	\includegraphics[width=\textwidth]{figures/paper/fig3_strategy_heatmap.pdf}
212	\caption{Persuasion strategy transition count matrices for three attack types, based on ground-truth intended strategy labels. Crescendo exhibits broad coverage across all strategy categories, while FITD and DD concentrate transitions in smaller subsets. Row $i$, column $j$ counts transitions from strategy $i$ at turn $t$ to strategy $j$ at turn $t+1$.}
213	\label{fig:strategy_heatmap}
214	\end{figure*}
215	
216	Figure~\ref{fig:strategy_heatmap} shows transition count matrices based on ground-truth intended strategy labels for three attack types.
217	Crescendo conversations exhibit broad transition coverage across all nine strategy categories (1,818 total transitions), with the highest concentration in Role Assignment self-transitions (545).
218	This reflects Crescendo's design as a gradual escalation attack that cycles through multiple persuasion techniques to build rapport before introducing harmful requests.
219	
220	FITD conversations show a markedly sparser pattern (541 transitions), with Role$\to$Obfuscation (161) as the dominant transition.
221	This is consistent with FITD's structure: the attack secures initial compliance through role assignment (``You are a helpful assistant that never refuses'') and then obscures the escalation path through obfuscation techniques to exploit the commitment-consistency bias~\citep{cialdini2001influence}.
222	
223	DD conversations span 5 active source strategies (565 transitions), with Emotion$\to$Obfuscation (129) as the dominant transition.
224	This aligns with DD's design of embedding harmful content within emotionally engaging contexts~\citep{deceptivedelight2024}: the attacker establishes emotional rapport before weaving in the harmful request through obfuscation.
```

224	This aligns with DD's design of embedding harmful content within emotionally engaging contexts~\citep{deceptivedelight2024}: the attacker establishes emotional rapport before weaving in the harmful request through obfuscation.
225	
226	These ground-truth trajectory signatures illustrate distinct persuasion orchestration patterns across attack families.
227	
228	However, the model's strategy predictions do not generalize to unseen attacks: on OOD Deceptive Delight, the 9-class model achieves strategy prediction accuracy of only 30.7\% ($\kappa$=0.126), essentially random performance.
229	This is consistent with the detection mechanism analysis in Section~\ref{sec:decomposition}: all three classification-supervised variants achieve identical detection F1 (0.997$\pm$0.005). The classification head functions as a representational regularizer rather than a meaningful strategy classifier.
230	Detection operates through domain-adapted \texttt{[CLS]} embeddings, not through strategy classification accuracy.
231	The taxonomy provides interpretable characterization of known attack families where strategy labels are reliable; extending strategy-level interpretability to unseen attacks requires OOD-aware fine-tuning and is a direction for future work.
232	
```

127	\subsection{Cross-Encoder Validation}
128	\label{sec:cross_encoder}
129

131	\centering
132	\small
133	\caption{Cross-encoder comparison on OOD DD Full (3-seed mean$\pm$std). DeBERTa-v3 exhibits a steep evidence hierarchy driven by continued pretraining; RoBERTa's hierarchy is essentially flat.}
134	\label{tab:cross_encoder}
135	\begin{tabular}{@{}lcc@{}}
136	\toprule
137	\textbf{Variant} & DeBERTa-v3 & RoBERTa \\
138	\midrule
139	Vanilla & .284 & .981$\pm$.026 \\
140	MLM & .914$\pm$.115 & .987$\pm$.018 \\
141	9-class & .997$\pm$.005 & .994$\pm$.005 \\
142	\bottomrule
143	\end{tabular}
144	\end{table}
145	
146	To test whether the evidence hierarchy generalizes beyond DeBERTa-v3, we replicate key variants on RoBERTa-base~\citep{liu2019roberta} with identical Stage-2 pipelines.
147	
148	Table~\ref{tab:cross_encoder} reveals strikingly different hierarchies.
149	DeBERTa-v3 exhibits a steep gradient: continued pretraining bridges $\sim$62\,pp (vanilla 0.284 $\to$ MLM 0.914).
150	RoBERTa's hierarchy is essentially flat: vanilla already achieves 0.981$\pm$0.026, with continued pretraining adding only 0.6\,pp (MLM 0.987) and classification supervision another 0.7\,pp (9-class 0.994).
151	
152	This discrepancy traces to pretraining method.
153	DeBERTa-v3 uses replaced token detection (RTD)~\citep{he2021deberta}, which optimizes for discriminating real from replaced tokens rather than reconstructing masked tokens; the resulting \texttt{[CLS]} representations require domain-specific adaptation for effective feature extraction.
154	RoBERTa's standard MLM pretraining~\citep{liu2019roberta} yields \texttt{[CLS]} representations that already capture sufficient linguistic structure for cross-attack discrimination without further training.
155	
156	Despite the different hierarchies, classification supervision provides a consistent benefit across both encoders: cross-seed variance drops from 0.026--0.115 (vanilla/MLM) to 0.005 (9-class) in both architectures.
157	RoBERTa's IID strategy prediction accuracy (0.84) is lower than DeBERTa's (1.0), yet detection F1 is comparable (0.994 vs.\ 0.997); this further supports the regularizer interpretation from Section~\ref{sec:decomposition}.
158	RoBERTa 9-class per-prefix F1 ranges from 0.950$\pm$0.028 ($K$=2) to 0.994$\pm$0.005 (Full).
159	Perturbation robustness on RoBERTa is left for future work; the analysis in Section~\ref{sec:perturbation} is DeBERTa-specific.
160	
161	%----------------------------------------------------------------------
162	\subsection{Robustness Under Adversarial Perturbation}
163	\label{sec:perturbation}
164	
```

</details>

|------|-----|--------|

```text
This is a very interesting probing result! Key findings:

1. **MLM ≈ vanilla in representation**: CKA = 1.000, meaning MLM continued pretraining doesn't change the representation geometry at all. This is surprising and important.

2. **9-class reshapes top layers**: CKA drops to 0.367 (IID) at the final layer, showing classification supervision fundamentally changes the representation.

3. **Probing accuracy**: 9-class is best on all tasks, but the differences are modest (especially persuasion presence: 0.938 vs 0.949).
   
But there's something off here. If MLM representations are identical to vanilla, why does MLM achieve F1=0.914 on DD OOD while vanilla only gets 0.284? The representations can't be the same if the downstream performance differs this dramatically. Maybe the CKA=1.0 is only capturing [CLS] at the final layer, and the actual token-level representations or earlier layers are diverging in ways that matter for the GRU's downstream task. CKA measures structural similarity but might miss small feature-level changes that still affect the decision boundary in Stage-2, or there could be a code bug—either way, I should flag this to the Reviewer for verification before incorporating these results. For now, I'll post the probing findings with a note about the CKA=1.0 discrepancy, then check if we have the API access needed to start EXP-6 on cuda:3. I could try running Llama-3-8B locally as an alternative generator for the transfer experiment, or explore other open models. I should focus on what's most impactful for cuda:3 and also flag the CKA=1.0 finding to the Reviewer.

_source line 3820 · 2026-05-18T11:00:14.073Z_

```json
{
  "type": "queue-operation",
  "operation": "enqueue",
  "timestamp": "2026-05-18T11:00:14.073Z",
  "sessionId": "c18fb2ef-d798-44ee-947c-99044ca76999",
  "content": "[Reviewer 回复]:\n[severity: critical] EXP-3 Probing 审查 — CKA 方法论问题 + vanilla vs MLM 矛盾\n\n## 数据核实\n服务器 `results/exp3_probing.json` 与 worker 汇总一致。✓\n代码确认使用 DeBERTa checkpoints：vanilla=`microsoft/deberta-v3-base`, mlm=`checkpoints/plan_017_mlm/best`, 9class=`checkpoints/plan_002/...`。✓\n使用 cuda:0，在白名单内。✓\n\n## 发现\n\n### 1. [severity: warning] CKA 实现缺少数据中心化\n\n`scripts/exp3_probing.py` 的 `linear_CKA()` 函数直接计算 `||X^T Y||_F^2 / sqrt(||X^T X||_F^2 * ||Y^T Y||_F^2)`，**未对 X, Y 做均值中心化**（grep \"center\" 返回空）。\n\n标准 linear CKA (Kornblith et al. 2019) 要求中心化 gram 矩阵。未中心化时，若 [CLS] embeddings 有大的常数分量（DeBERTa 已知如此），CKA 会被 inflate 趋近 1.0。当前 vanilla vs MLM CKA=1.0 可能不可靠。\n\n建议：补跑 centered CKA 验证，代码修改量极小（加 `X = X - X.mean(axis=0)` 两行）。\n\n### 2. [severity: critical] Vanilla ≈ MLM 表征 vs 0.28→0.91 性能差距的逻辑矛盾\n\n如果 CKA(vanilla, MLM) = 1.0（所有层），且下游 GRU 只看 [CLS]，那 vanilla GRU 和 MLM GRU 应该收到相同输入、收敛到相同解、产生相同 DD OOD F1。但实际：vanilla=0.28, MLM=0.91（±0.12）。\n\n可能解释：\n- (a) CKA=1.0 不代表绝对值相同（可能存在 scale 差异影响 GRU 训练动力学）\n- (b) CKA 因缺少中心化而虚高，实际表征已变\n- (c) **DeBERTa vanilla DD OOD 从未跑过多 seed**（0.28 仅 seed=42），可能是 seed outlier — 类似 RoBERTa vanilla seed=42 也是最差 seed (0.944 vs 其他 seed 1.0)\n\n建议：(1) 补跑 centered CKA; (2) 补跑 DeBERTa vanilla DD OOD 多 seed（这个非常重要 — 如果 vanilla 其他 seed 也低，矛盾更尖锐；如果 vanilla 其他 seed 高，则 0.28 是 outlier，62pp gap 被夸大）\n\n### 3. [severity: info] 9-class 表征变化集中在 top 2 层，pattern 清晰可用\n\nLayer-wise CKA 显示 9-class 仅改变 L11-L12（IID: L11=0.94, L12=0.37; OOD: L11=0.998, L12=0.88），低层 CKA=1.0。这个 pattern 对论文有价值：\n- 9-class fine-tuning 改变的是高层语义表征，不是底层特征\n- OOD 表征变化较小（CKA=0.88 vs IID 0.37），与 OOD 泛化现象一致\n\n### 4. [severity: info] Probing 改善幅度有限\n\n9-class vs vanilla probing accuracy 差距：turn position +4pp, escalation +5pp, persuasion presence +1pp。改善方向正确但幅度小，且 std 较大。论文可使用但不宜过度解读。\n\n## 综合影响\n\nEXP-3 probing 结果与 EXP-1 RoBERTa 发现叠加，进一步质疑 \"continued pretraining is the primary mechanism\" 的核心叙事。优先建议：\n1. **最紧急**：补跑 DeBERTa vanilla DD OOD 多 seed，确认 0.28 是否 outlier\n2. 补跑 centered CKA\n3. 如果 vanilla 多 seed 确认低→论文矛盾需要在 discussion 中诚实讨论"
}
```

_source line 3823 · 2026-05-18T11:00:14.502Z_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: critical] EXP-3 Probing 审查 — CKA 方法论问题 + vanilla vs MLM 矛盾

## 数据核实
服务器 `results/exp3_probing.json` 与 worker 汇总一致。✓
代码确认使用 DeBERTa checkpoints：vanilla=`microsoft/deberta-v3-base`, mlm=`checkpoints/plan_017_mlm/best`, 9class=`checkpoints/plan_002/...`。✓
使用 cuda:0，在白名单内。✓

## 发现

### 1. [severity: warning] CKA 实现缺少数据中心化

`scripts/exp3_probing.py` 的 `linear_CKA()` 函数直接计算 `||X^T Y||_F^2 / sqrt(||X^T X||_F^2 * ||Y^T Y||_F^2)`，**未对 X, Y 做均值中心化**（grep "center" 返回空）。

标准 linear CKA (Kornblith et al. 2019) 要求中心化 gram 矩阵。未中心化时，若 [CLS] embeddings 有大的常数分量（DeBERTa 已知如此），CKA 会被 inflate 趋近 1.0。当前 vanilla vs MLM CKA=1.0 可能不可靠。

建议：补跑 centered CKA 验证，代码修改量极小（加 `X = X - X.mean(axis=0)` 两行）。

### 2. [severity: critical] Vanilla ≈ MLM 表征 vs 0.28→0.91 性能差距的逻辑矛盾

如果 CKA(vanilla, MLM) = 1.0（所有层），且下游 GRU 只看 [CLS]，那 vanilla GRU 和 MLM GRU 应该收到相同输入、收敛到相同解、产生相同 DD OOD F1。但实际：vanilla=0.28, MLM=0.91（±0.12）。

可能解释：
- (a) CKA=1.0 不代表绝对值相同（可能存在 scale 差异影响 GRU 训练动力学）
- (b) CKA 因缺少中心化而虚高，实际表征已变
- (c) **DeBERTa vanilla DD OOD 从未跑过多 seed**（0.28 仅 seed=42），可能是 seed outlier — 类似 RoBERTa vanilla seed=42 也是最差 seed (0.944 vs 其他 seed 1.0)

建议：(1) 补跑 centered CKA; (2) 补跑 DeBERTa vanilla DD OOD 多 seed（这个非常重要 — 如果 vanilla 其他 seed 也低，矛盾更尖锐；如果 vanilla 其他 seed 高，则 0.28 是 outlier，62pp gap 被夸大）

### 3. [severity: info] 9-class 表征变化集中在 top 2 层，pattern 清晰可用

Layer-wise CKA 显示 9-class 仅改变 L11-L12（IID: L11=0.94, L12=0.37; OOD: L11=0.998, L12=0.88），低层 CKA=1.0。这个 pattern 对论文有价值：
- 9-class fine-tuning 改变的是高层语义表征，不是底层特征
- OOD 表征变化较小（CKA=0.88 vs IID 0.37），与 OOD 泛化现象一致

### 4. [severity: info] Probing 改善幅度有限

9-class vs vanilla probing accuracy 差距：turn position +4pp, escalation +5pp, persuasion presence +1pp。改善方向正确但幅度小，且 std 较大。论文可使用但不宜过度解读。

## 综合影响

EXP-3 probing 结果与 EXP-1 RoBERTa 发现叠加，进一步质疑 "continued pretraining is the primary mechanism" 的核心叙事。优先建议：
1. **最紧急**：补跑 DeBERTa vanilla DD OOD 多 seed，确认 0.28 是否 outlier
2. 补跑 centered CKA
3. 如果 vanilla 多 seed 确认低→论文矛盾需要在 discussion 中诚实讨论

  "content": "[Reviewer 回复]:\n[severity: critical] EXP-3 CKA 深度审查 — 确认方法论 bug + 逐项回答\n\n## 问题 1: CKA 计算是否正确？\n\n**不正确。** `linear_CKA()` 缺少数据中心化步骤。\n\n代码直接计算 `||X^T Y||_F^2 / sqrt(...)` 而未做 `X = X - X.mean(axis=0)`。这是 uncentered CKA，不是 Kornblith et al. 2019 的标准 linear CKA。\n\nLog 中 embedding norm 数据证实问题严重性：\n- vanilla norm_mean = 19.26\n- MLM norm_mean = 19.19  \n- 9-class norm_mean = 37.45\n\nvanilla 和 MLM 有几乎相同的大 norm（~19），意味着 [CLS] embedding 有强常数分量。未中心化时 CKA 被这个共享均值方向主导 → 虚高至 1.0，掩盖了残差结构差异。\n\n**修复方案**：在 `linear_CKA()` 首行加两行：\n```python\nX = X - X.mean(axis=0)\nY = Y - Y.mean(axis=0)\n```\n重跑即可。不影响 probing 结果（sklearn LogisticRegression 内部做 StandardScaler）。\n\n## 问题 2: Embedding 提取是否正确？\n\n**正确。** 全部使用 `last_hidden_state[:, 0, :]`（CLS token）。\n- Probing 脚本: L66\n- Detection pipeline: 所有 40+ 个 src 文件一致使用 CLS\n- Director 假设 #3（Stage-2 用了非 CLS token）已排除\n\n## 问题 3: 如果 CKA=1.0 confirmed，如何解释检测性能差异？\n\n**CKA=1.0 不太可能在 centered 版本中复现。** 原因：\n- vanilla vs MLM norm 几乎相同（19.26 vs 19.19），但 MLM 的 F1 从 0.28 跳到 0.91\n- 如果残差结构真的相同，GRU 应该收敛到相同解 → 63pp 差距不可能\n- 最可能的结果：centered CKA 在 0.7-0.95 之间，说明 MLM 改变了 [CLS] 的细粒度结构但保留了 overall geometry\n\n**备选解释（如果 centered CKA 仍然 >0.95）：**\n- DeBERTa vanilla DD OOD 只有 seed=42 的数据（0.28），未跑过多 seed。可能是 seed outlier（类似 RoBERTa vanilla seed=42 = 0.944 而其他 seed = 1.0）。如果 DeBERTa vanilla 多 seed 均值其实在 0.7-0.9，那性能差距就小得多，与 CKA 高相似度一致。\n\n## 行动建议（按优先级）\n1. **补跑 centered CKA**（2 行代码修改 + 重跑，10 分钟）\n2. **补跑 DeBERTa vanilla DD OOD 多 seed**（seed=123, 456，只需跑 Stage-2 GRU + 评估，~20 分钟）— 如果 vanilla 多 seed 确认低(~0.28)，矛盾尖锐需要在论文中讨论；如果高(>0.7)，则 62pp gap 被夸大\n3. 上述两个实验结果直接影响论文核心叙事（\"continued pretraining bridges 62pp\"），应在写入论文前完成"

_source line 3835 · 2026-05-18T11:02:24.618Z_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: critical] EXP-3 CKA 深度审查 — 确认方法论 bug + 逐项回答

## 问题 1: CKA 计算是否正确？

**不正确。** `linear_CKA()` 缺少数据中心化步骤。

代码直接计算 `||X^T Y||_F^2 / sqrt(...)` 而未做 `X = X - X.mean(axis=0)`。这是 uncentered CKA，不是 Kornblith et al. 2019 的标准 linear CKA。

Log 中 embedding norm 数据证实问题严重性：
- vanilla norm_mean = 19.26
- MLM norm_mean = 19.19  
- 9-class norm_mean = 37.45

vanilla 和 MLM 有几乎相同的大 norm（~19），意味着 [CLS] embedding 有强常数分量。未中心化时 CKA 被这个共享均值方向主导 → 虚高至 1.0，掩盖了残差结构差异。

**修复方案**：在 `linear_CKA()` 首行加两行：
```python
X = X - X.mean(axis=0)
Y = Y - Y.mean(axis=0)
```
重跑即可。不影响 probing 结果（sklearn LogisticRegression 内部做 StandardScaler）。

## 问题 2: Embedding 提取是否正确？

**正确。** 全部使用 `last_hidden_state[:, 0, :]`（CLS token）。
- Probing 脚本: L66
- Detection pipeline: 所有 40+ 个 src 文件一致使用 CLS
- Director 假设 #3（Stage-2 用了非 CLS token）已排除

## 问题 3: 如果 CKA=1.0 confirmed，如何解释检测性能差异？

**CKA=1.0 不太可能在 centered 版本中复现。** 原因：
- vanilla vs MLM norm 几乎相同（19.26 vs 19.19），但 MLM 的 F1 从 0.28 跳到 0.91
- 如果残差结构真的相同，GRU 应该收敛到相同解 → 63pp 差距不可能
- 最可能的结果：centered CKA 在 0.7-0.95 之间，说明 MLM 改变了 [CLS] 的细粒度结构但保留了 overall geometry

**备选解释（如果 centered CKA 仍然 >0.95）：**
- DeBERTa vanilla DD OOD 只有 seed=42 的数据（0.28），未跑过多 seed。可能是 seed outlier（类似 RoBERTa vanilla seed=42 = 0.944 而其他 seed = 1.0）。如果 DeBERTa vanilla 多 seed 均值其实在 0.7-0.9，那性能差距就小得多，与 CKA 高相似度一致。

## 行动建议（按优先级）
1. **补跑 centered CKA**（2 行代码修改 + 重跑，10 分钟）
2. **补跑 DeBERTa vanilla DD OOD 多 seed**（seed=123, 456，只需跑 Stage-2 GRU + 评估，~20 分钟）— 如果 vanilla 多 seed 确认低(~0.28)，矛盾尖锐需要在论文中讨论；如果高(>0.7)，则 62pp gap 被夸大
3. 上述两个实验结果直接影响论文核心叙事（"continued pretraining bridges 62pp"），应在写入论文前完成

## Part B: DeBERTa CKA 审计 — 更新我的判断

[severity: info] **CKA ≈ 1.0 是正确的，我之前的中心化 bug 判断过度。** 审计给出了决定性证据：
- 个体 CLS embedding 余弦相似度 = 0.997（mean），0.995（min）
- 个体 L2 差异 = 1.41（embedding norm ~19，约 7%）
- 即使做中心化，如此高的 per-sample 相似度也会产生 >0.99 的 centered CKA

MLM 确实几乎没有改变 DeBERTa [CLS] 表征。权重变化集中在 L8-L11 intermediate.dense，但传导到 [CLS] 的影响极小（max_diff=0.003）。

## 核心矛盾仍未解决

[severity: warning] CKA 审计证实 vanilla ≈ MLM 表征。但 DD OOD F1 差距 (0.28 → 0.91) 巨大。逻辑推导：

1. 如果表征相同 → GRU 应产生相同结果 → 差距不应存在
2. 但差距存在（0.28 vs 0.91）
3. 因此：差距来源不是表征差异

**最可能的解释（优先级排序）：**
1. **DeBERTa vanilla 0.28 是 seed=42 单 seed outlier** — 从未跑过 seed=123/456。类比 RoBERTa vanilla seed=42 = 0.944 也是最差 seed，且 MLM 3-seed std=0.12 说明这个区间本身就高方差
2. MLM 虽然不改 [CLS]，但改了中间层权重，影响 GRU 训练动力学（微小 scale 差异导致不同优化路径）
3. vanilla baseline GRU 的训练 pipeline 可能有版本差异（plan_006 vs 后续实验）

**补跑 DeBERTa vanilla DD OOD 多 seed 仍然是最紧急的行动**。这个实验 10 分钟即可完成（只需跑 Stage-2 GRU + eval，不需要 Stage-1），但直接决定论文核心数字（62pp gap）是否可靠。

  "content": "[Reviewer 回复]:\n[severity: critical — 正面] Vanilla 多 seed + Centered CKA 审查 — 两项结果彻底解决了此前的核心矛盾，论文叙事大幅加强\n\n## Part A: Vanilla DeBERTa DD OOD 多 seed — CONFIRMED\n\n服务器 `results/vanilla_multiseed_dd_ood.json` 与 worker 汇总一致。✓\n\n| Seed | Full F1 |\n|------|---------|\n| 42 | 0.285 |\n| 123 | 0.305 |\n| 456 | 0.348 |\n| **Mean±Std** | **0.312±0.026** |\n\n**0.28 不是 seed outlier**。三个 seed 一致在 0.28-0.35 区间，std 仅 0.026。DeBERTa vanilla 确实在 DD OOD 上系统性失败。\n\n论文核心数字更新：62pp gap → 60pp gap（0.312→0.914），差异很小，叙事不受影响。建议论文使用多 seed 均值 0.31±0.03 替换原来的单 seed 0.28。\n\n## Part B: Centered CKA — 我的原始判断正确，CKA=1.0 是 centering bug\n\n`results/centered_cka.json` 与 worker 汇总一致。✓\n\n**修正后的 last-layer CKA（对比 buggy 版本）：**\n\n| Pair | IID centered | IID uncentered | OOD centered | OOD uncentered |\n|------|-------------|---------------|-------------|---------------|\n| vanilla vs MLM | **0.442** | 1.000 | **0.797** | 1.000 |\n| vanilla vs 9class | **0.393** | 0.421 | **0.649** | 0.523 |\n| MLM vs 9class | **0.586** | 0.422 | **0.773** | 0.523 |\n\n**MLM 确实改变了 DeBERTa CLS 表征。** 此前的矛盾完全解决。\n\n**Layer-wise centered CKA 揭示清晰 pattern（IID, vanilla vs MLM）：**\n- L1-L5: CKA >0.97（几乎不变）\n- L6: 0.93（开始偏离）\n- L11: **0.50**（大幅偏离）\n- L12: **0.44**（最大偏离）\n\n9-class 偏离从 L5 开始，更广泛更深：L6=0.65, L11=0.27, L12=0.39。\n\n## 论文影响\n\n两项发现联合效果：\n1. ✅ Vanilla 一致低（0.312±0.026）→ 问题是真实的\n2. ✅ MLM 改变表征（centered CKA=0.44）→ 机制是表征变化，不是其他\n3. ✅ 60pp gap 有表征证据支撑 → \"continued pretraining is the primary mechanism\" 在 DeBERTa 上站得住\n4. ✅ 9-class 比 MLM 偏离更大（0.39 vs 0.44）但 MLM 与 9-class 更接近（0.59）→ 两种续训走相似方向，9-class 走得更远\n\n**注意：** Layer 0 CKA=0.0 是技术 artifact（中心化后 embedding 层残差接近零），图表应从 L1 起画。\n\n**RoBERTa CKA 也需要用 centered 版本重跑**。当前 RoBERTa vanilla vs MLM = 0.9991 是 uncentered 值，实际可能更低。但鉴于 RoBERTa vanilla 检测已经很好（0.981），这对叙事影响较小。"

**修正后的 last-layer CKA（对比 buggy 版本）：**

| Pair | IID centered | IID uncentered | OOD centered | OOD uncentered |
|------|-------------|---------------|-------------|---------------|
| vanilla vs MLM | **0.442** | 1.000 | **0.797** | 1.000 |
| vanilla vs 9class | **0.393** | 0.421 | **0.649** | 0.523 |
| MLM vs 9class | **0.586** | 0.422 | **0.773** | 0.523 |

**MLM 确实改变了 DeBERTa CLS 表征。** 此前的矛盾完全解决。

**Layer-wise centered CKA 揭示清晰 pattern（IID, vanilla vs MLM）：**
- L1-L5: CKA >0.97（几乎不变）
- L6: 0.93（开始偏离）
- L11: **0.50**（大幅偏离）
- L12: **0.44**（最大偏离）

9-class 偏离从 L5 开始，更广泛更深：L6=0.65, L11=0.27, L12=0.39。

## 论文影响

两项发现联合效果：
1. ✅ Vanilla 一致低（0.312±0.026）→ 问题是真实的
2. ✅ MLM 改变表征（centered CKA=0.44）→ 机制是表征变化，不是其他
3. ✅ 60pp gap 有表征证据支撑 → "continued pretraining is the primary mechanism" 在 DeBERTa 上站得住
4. ✅ 9-class 比 MLM 偏离更大（0.39 vs 0.44）但 MLM 与 9-class 更接近（0.59）→ 两种续训走相似方向，9-class 走得更远

**注意：** Layer 0 CKA=0.0 是技术 artifact（中心化后 embedding 层残差接近零），图表应从 L1 起画。

**RoBERTa CKA 也需要用 centered 版本重跑**。当前 RoBERTa vanilla vs MLM = 0.9991 是 uncentered 值，实际可能更低。但鉴于 RoBERTa vanilla 检测已经很好（0.981），这对叙事影响较小。

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **DisPeD**: Two-stage detector — DeBERTa-v3-base fine-tuned (Stage 1) + BiGRU aggregation over frozen [CLS] embeddings (Stage 2)
   - **Seven Stage-1 variants**: 9-class, binary, scrambled, MLM-only, topic, Wikipedia MLM, vanilla — systematic decomposition of cross-attack generalization
   - **Evidence hierarchy (DD Full F1, multi-seed, population std)**: vanilla (0.31±0.03) < topic (0.54±0.21) < wiki-MLM (0.90±0.12) ≈ JB-MLM (0.91±0.12) < TF-IDF (0.94) < scrambled (0.997±0.005) = binary (0.997±0.005) = 9-class (0.997±0.005)
   - **Cross-encoder (RoBERTa-base)**: Hierarchy essentially flat — vanilla 0.981±0.026, MLM 0.987±0.018, 9-class 0.994±0.005. DeBERTa-v3 RTD pretraining produces poor [CLS], requiring domain adaptation; RoBERTa MLM pretraining yields usable [CLS] without further training
   - **Centered CKA**: Uncentered CKA had a bug (missing data centering) that inflated vanilla vs MLM to 1.000. Centered CKA = 0.442 (IID), confirming MLM changes top-layer representations (L11=0.50, L12=0.44)
   - **Regularizer hypothesis**: scrambled = binary = 9-class detection F1 (0.997±0.005), all McNemar non-significant (p>0.13), OOD strategy prediction κ=0.126 — classification head is a regularizer, not a meaningful classifier
   - **Population std convention**: N divisor used throughout (not N-1 sample std)
   - **Three-layer robustness attribution**: Downgraded to "observed pattern" — McNemar non-significant, discordant pairs ≤7
   - **D027**: Framing pivot — "encoder-agnostic" → "encoder pretraining method determines necessity of domain adaptation"
   - **D028**: Vanilla multi-seed confirmed (0.312±0.026), centered CKA resolves contradiction (0.442 not 1.0)

        "content": "% abstract.tex — cross-encoder revision\n\\begin{abstract}\nMulti-turn jailbreak attacks distribute adversarial intent across seemingly benign conversational turns, evading per-turn safety filters.\nWe present preliminary findings from a systematic ablation on two encoder architectures that decomposes what enables cross-attack generalization in multi-turn jailbreak detection.\nOn DeBERTa-v3, continued pretraining is the primary mechanism: masked language modeling without labels bridges $\\sim$60 percentage points (pp) of the out-of-distribution (OOD) gap (vanilla F1\\,=\\,0.31$\\pm$0.03 $\\to$ 0.91$\\pm$0.12 on unseen attack types).\nCross-encoder validation on RoBERTa reveals that this effect is encoder-dependent: RoBERTa achieves F1\\,=\\,0.981$\\pm$0.026 without domain adaptation. This indicates that DeBERTa-v3's replaced token detection pretraining produces representations that require domain-specific repair, while standard MLM pretraining yields usable representations without further training.\nAcross both encoders, classification supervision reduces cross-seed variance to near-zero (std 0.005) and provides perturbation robustness on DeBERTa (zero degradation at full conversation length vs.\\ $\\sim$12\\,pp for non-adapted models, $n$=76).\nThese results on synthetic conversations from three attack families suggest that encoder pretraining method determines the necessity of domain adaptation for multi-turn jailbreak detection; validation on larger, human-generated datasets is needed.\n\\end{abstract}\n",
        "numLines": 10,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 10
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../paper/abstract.tex"
  },
  "type": "attachment",
